Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the probabilities object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 139. The moduli space has dimension at most 886 with 5 connected components.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^886(S, F) is trivial. QED.
